provider of power tools and outdoor power equipment (“OPE”).      設備（「OPE」）的全球供應商。我們對創新的高度專

under a portfolio of well-recognized brands, which are tailored   為滿足全球終端用戶的多樣化需求而量身定制。我

2025 is a challenging year for us. Following a significant       2025年對我們而言是充滿挑戰的一年。繼2024年大
rebound and growth in 2024, we faced a direct and severe         幅反彈和增長後，我們於2025年面臨中美關稅問題
impact from tariff-related issues between China and the United   的直接且嚴重影響。儘管市場環境充滿挑戰，本集
States in 2025. Despite the challenging market conditions,       團於2025年仍保持盈利，年內錄得純利97.7百萬美
our Group remained profitable in 2025, recording a net profit    元。反映我們產品在主要市場的核心競爭力的關鍵
of US$97.7 million for the year. Key indicators reflecting       指標亦保持韌性，使我們對市場最終復甦及本集團
the core competitiveness of our products in our principal        重返增長軌道充滿信心。
markets also remained resilient, giving us confidence in the
market’s eventual recovery and our Group’s return to a growth
trajectory.


Against a backdrop of overall market weakness, the vast          在整體市場疲軟的背景下，泉峰OBM 及 ODM業務
majority of Chervon’s product lines across both our OBM          的絕大部分產品線實現客戶端終端銷售正增長，
and ODM businesses achieved positive POS growth at the           EGO 品牌錄得兩位數的終端銷售增長。這凸顯我們
customer end, with the EGO brand recording double-digit          創新能力的韌性及產品的競爭力。全年期間，我們
POS growth. This underscores the resilience of our innovation    持續致力於為客戶及終端用戶服務，並不斷夯實未
capabilities and the competitiveness of our products.            來增長的基礎。
Throughout the year, we remained committed to serving our
customers and end users while continuing to strengthen the
foundation for future growth.

MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
管理層討論與分析




BUSINESS REVIEW                                                   業務概覽
In 2025, our revenue recorded a periodic decline, representing    於2025年，由於在中美關稅緊張局勢導致的經濟不
a decrease of 8.2% to US$1,627.8 million, as our customers        確定性增加的情況下，我們的客戶採取更審慎的採
adopted more prudent procurement strategies amid the              購策略，因此我們的收入錄得階段性下跌，即減少
heightened economic uncertainty resulting from the China-U.S.     8.2%至1,627.8百萬美元。
tariff tensions.


Our net profit also experienced a decrease of 13.3% as            我們的純利亦較2024年減少13.3%，主要由於我們
compared to 2024 primarily driven by the decrease in our          的收入減少及毛利率下降。
revenue and gross profit margin.


Revenue from our outdoor power equipment (“OPE”) segment          戶 外 動 力 設 備（「 O P E 」）分 部 的 收 入 增 長 0 . 1 % ，
increased by 0.1%, and revenue from our power tools               而電動工具分部的收入則減少 18.3%。2025 年，
segment decreased by 18.3%. Revenue from our original             原始品牌製造（「OBM」）業務收入佔我們總收入的
brand manufacturing (“OBM”) business accounted for 76.7%          76.7%。按地區劃分，來自北美及中國的業務分別
of our total revenue in 2025. By region, our business from        減少11.5%及3.8%，而歐洲及世界其他地區的業務
North America and China decreased by 11.5% and 3.8%,              分別增長0.9%及6.8%。
respectively, and business from Europe and the rest of the

Exposure to Fluctuations in Foreign Exchange Rates                  外匯匯率波動風險

The Group manufactures and sells its products in many               本集團於全球多個國家製造及銷售其產品。因此，
countries throughout the world. As a result, there is exposure      由於本集團進行的交易與投資是以多種貨幣計值，
to foreign currency risk as the Group enters into transactions      故面臨外幣風險。例如，貨幣匯率變動可能影響本
and makes investments denominated in multiple currencies.           集團及其競爭對手於同一市場銷售產品的相對價格
For example, changes in currency exchange rates may affect          及本集團營運所需的產品及服務成本。本集團的主
the relative prices at which the Group and its competitors sell     要風險敞口為美元、歐元及人民幣。本集團面臨著
products in the same market and the costs of products and           將其附屬公司的資產負債表及收益表換算為美元所

 DIVIDEND POLICY                                                     股息政策
 Pursuant to the dividend policy adopted by the Company (the         根據本公司採納的股息政策（「股息政策」），股息宣
 “Dividend Policy”), the declaration of dividends is subject to      派由董事會酌情釐定及（如需要）須經股東批准。董
 the discretion of the Board, and, if necessary, the approval of     事會考慮宣派及派發股息時，須考慮本集團的經營
 the Shareholders. In considering the declaration and payment        業績、現金流量、財務狀況、本公司派發股息的法
 of dividends, the Board shall take into account the Group’s         定及監管限制、本公司的資本需求、未來業務計劃
 results of operations, cash flows, financial condition, statutory   及前景以及董事會可能認為相關的任何其他因素。
 and regulatory restrictions on the payment of dividends by          任何股息的宣派及派發以及金額亦將須遵守本公司
 the Company, the Company’s capital requirements, future             組織章程細則（「組織章程細則」）及所有適用的法律
 business plans and prospects and any other factors that the         及法規。董事可不時重新評估股息政策。
 Board may consider relevant. Any declaration and payment,
 as well as the amount, of any dividend will also be subject to
 the articles of association of the Company (the “Articles of
 Association”) and all applicable laws and regulations. The
 Directors may reassess the Dividend Policy from time to time.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                      企業管治常規
The Company strives to attain and maintain high standards           本公司相信有效的企業管治架構是提高及保障股東
of corporate governance best suited to the needs and                及其他持份者權益與提升股東價值的基本要素，因
interests of the Group as it believes that an effective corporate   此致力達致及維持最符合本集團需要及利益的高水

safeguarding interests of Shareholders and other stakeholders       原則及常規，強調具備一個優秀的董事會、有效的
and enhancing Shareholder value. Accordingly, the Company           風險管理及內部控制系統、嚴格的披露常規、透明
has adopted and applied corporate governance principles             度及問責性，以及與股東及其他持份者進行有效的
and practices that emphasize a quality board of Directors,          溝通及互動。此外，本集團致力不斷提升該等標準
effective risk management and internal control systems,             及常規，並建立穩健的合規及道德管治文化，以配
stringent disclosure practices, transparency and accountability     合本集團的業務營運及常規。
as well as effective communication and engagement with
Shareholders and other stakeholders. It is, in addition,
committed to continuously enhancing these standards and
practices and inculcating a robust culture of compliance and
ethical governance underlying the business operations and
practices across the Group.

Reporting Period. The amendments to the CG Code came                治守則》的規定將適用於本公司2025年7月1日或之

 THE BOARD                                                             董事會
 Corporate Culture and Strategy                                        企業文化及策略

 The Group instills a culture that respects and promotes               本集團推崇尊重及激發創新、交流想法的機會及交
 creativity, opportunities to exchange ideas and cross-fertilization   叉分享創新進步及解決方案的文化。董事會為本公
 of innovative advancements and solutions. The Board sets              司的企業文化定調，並以本集團於所有層面合法、
 the tone and shapes the corporate culture of the Company,             合乎道德及負責的核心價值觀為基礎。董事會在界
 which is underpinned by the core values of acting lawfully,           定本集團的宗旨、價值觀及策略方向，以及培育具
 ethically and responsibly across all levels of the Group.             前瞻性、擁抱變化及著重競爭力的文化方面發揮領
 The Board plays a leading role in defining the purpose,               導作用。本集團在營運常規、工作場所政策及常規
 values and strategic direction of the Group and in fostering          以及與持份者的關係中建立及貫徹反映所需文化。
 a culture that is forward-looking, change-embracing and               長期以來，董事會對文化的監督包含一系列措施及
 competitiveness-focused. The desired culture is developed and         工具，包括員工參與、員工留任及培訓、嚴格的財
 reflected consistently in the operating practices of the Group,       務報告、有效及可用的舉報框架、法律及監管合規
 workplace policies and practices as well as relations with            （包括遵守行為守則及集團政策）以及員工安全、福
 stakeholders. The Board’s oversight of culture encompasses            祉及支持。經考慮一系列情況下的企業文化，董事
a range of measures and tools over time, including workforce           會認為本集團的文化與宗旨、價值觀及策略一致。
engagement, employee retention and training, stringent financial
reporting, effective and accessible whistleblowing framework,

 Chairman and Chief Executive Officer                                主席及行政總裁
 Provision C.2.1 of the CG Code requires that the roles of           企業管治守則第C.2.1條要求主席與行政總裁的角色
 chairman and chief executive officer be separate and not be         應有區分，並不應由一人同時兼任。
 performed by the same individual.


 However, the Company does not have separate chairman                然而，本公司並無區分主席及行政總裁，而主席及
 and chief executive officer roles, and the responsibilities of      行政總裁的職責均由潘龍泉先生承擔。董事會相
 both chairman and chief executive officer vest in Mr. Pan           信，由同一人兼任主席及行政總裁的職責，可確保
 Longquan. The Board believes that vesting the responsibilities      本集團內部領導貫徹一致，使本集團的整體策略規
 of both chairman and chief executive officer in the same            劃更有效及更具效率。此外，董事會合共七名董事
 person has the benefit of ensuring the consistent leadership        中有三名獨立非執行董事，董事會內將有足夠的獨
 within the Group and enables more effective and efficient           立意見，以保障本公司及股東的整體利益。
 overall strategic planning of the Group. Besides, with three
 independent non-executive Directors out of a total of seven
 Directors on the Board, there will be sufficient independent
 voice within the Board to protect the interests of the Company
 and the Shareholders as a whole.

Therefore, the Board considers that the balance of power and        因此，董事會認為，現行安排不會損害權力與權限
authority for the present arrangement will not be impaired and      之間的平衡，而此架構將使本公司能夠迅速及有效
this structure will enable the Company to make and implement        地作出及實施決策。董事會將繼續進行檢討，並會
decisions promptly and effectively. The Board will continue         在計及本集團整體情況後考慮於適當時候將董事會
to review and consider splitting the roles of chairman of the       主席與本公司行政總裁的角色分開。

directors are responsible for assessing the Group’s ability        續經營的能力，並在適用情況下披露與持續經營有
to continue as a going concern, disclosing, as applicable,         關的事項，以及使用持續經營為會計基礎，除非董
matters related to going concern and using the going concern       事有意將   貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別無其他實
basis of accounting unless the directors either intend to          際的替代方案。
liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or have no realistic

      a limited liability company with its registered office at          事處位於香港九龍荔枝角長順街 7 號西頓中
      Unit 04, 22/F, Saxon Tower, 7 Cheung Shun Street, Lai              心22樓04室。本公司股份於2021年12月30
      Chi Kok, Kowloon, Hong Kong. The Company’s shares                  日於香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市。
      were listed on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange of
      Hong Kong Limited on December 30, 2021.


      The Company is an investment holding company. The                  本公司為投資控股公司。本公司及其附屬公
      Company and its subsidiaries (together, “the Group”)               司（統稱「本集團」）主要從事電動工具、戶外
      are principally engaged in researching, developing,                動力設備及相關產品的研究、開發、製造、

      for power tools, outdoor power equipment and related               料載於附註16。

 (b)      Basis of preparation of the financial statements              (b)   財務報表編製基準

          with HKFRS Accounting Standards requires                            報表要求管理層須作出判斷、估計及假設，

          assumptions that affect the application of policies and             及所呈報的資產、負債、收入及開支金額。
          reported amounts of assets, liabilities, income and                 該等估計及相關假設乃基於過往經驗及在有
          expenses. The estimates and associated assumptions                  關情況下被認為屬合理的各項其他因素，而
          are based on historical experience and various other                所得結果乃用作判斷顯然無法輕易通過其他
          factors that are believed to be reasonable under the                來源獲得有關資產與負債賬面值的依據。實
          circumstances, the results of which form the basis of               際結果或有別於該等估計。
          making the judgements about carrying values of assets
          and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other
          sources. Actual results may differ from these estimates.


          The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed               該等估計及相關假設被持續審閱。若會計估
          on an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates              計的修訂僅影響作出修訂的期間，將會於該
          are recognized in the period in which the estimate is               期間確認，若修訂影響當期及未來期間，則
          revised if the revision affects only that period, or in the         會於作出修訂及其後期間確認。
          period of the revision and future periods if the revision

(d)   Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                   (d)    附屬公司及非控股權益

      Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The              附屬公司乃由本集團所控制的實體。當本集
      Group controls an entity when it is exposed to, or has              團能透過其參與承擔或享有實體可變回報的
      rights to, variable returns from its involvement with the           風險或權利，並能夠向實體使用其權力影響
      entity and has the ability to affect those returns through          回報金額，即對該實體擁有控制權。附屬公
      its power over the entity. The financial statements of              司的財務報表自控制開始之日直至控制終止
      subsidiaries are included in the consolidated financial             之日併入綜合財務報表。
      statements from the date on which control commences
      until the date on which control ceases.


      Intra-group balances and transactions, and any                      集團內公司間結餘及交易，以及集團內公司
      unrealised income and expenses (except for foreign                  間交易所產生的任何未變現收入及開支（外
      currency transaction gains or losses) arising from                  幣交易收益或虧損除外）均予以抵銷。集團
      intra-group transactions, are eliminated. Unrealised                內公司間交易所產生的未變現虧損的抵銷方
      losses resulting from intra-group transactions are                  法與未變現收益相同，惟僅限於並無減值跡
      eliminated in the same way as unrealised gains,                     象的情況下。
      but only to the extent that there is no evidence of
      impairment.


      Changes in the Group’s interests in a subsidiary that               本集團將不導致喪失控股權的附屬公司權益
      do not result in a loss of control are accounted for as             的變動按權益交易方式入賬。
      equity transactions.

          is recognized in profit or loss. Any interest retained in           於喪失控股權之日仍保留於該前度附屬公司

          group classified as held for sale).


 (e)      Associates                                                    (e)   聯營公司

          An associate is an entity in which the Group or the                 聯營公司為本集團或本公司對其有重大影響
          Company has significant influence, but not control or               力但對其財務及經營政策並無控制權或共同
          joint control, over the financial and operating policies.           控制權的實體。


          An interest in an associate is accounted for using the              於聯營公司的權益按權益法列賬，除非其分
          equity method, unless it is classified as held for sale (or         類為持作出售（或計入分類為持作出售的出
          included in a disposal group classified as held for sale).          售組別）。其初步按成本確認，包括交易成
          They are initially recognized at cost, which includes               本。其後，綜合財務報表包括本集團應佔該
          transaction costs. Subsequently, the consolidated                   等 被 投 資 公 司 的 損 益 及 其 他 全 面 收 益（「 其
          financial statements include the Group’s share of the               他全面收益」），直至重大影響力終止當日為
          profit or loss and other comprehensive income (“OCI”)               止。
          of those investees, until the date on which significant
          influence ceases.


          When the Group’s share of losses exceeds its interest               當本集團分佔虧損超過其於聯營公司的權益
          in the associate, the Group’s interest is reduced to                時，本集團的權益減少至零且終止確認進一
          nil and recognition of further losses is discontinued               步虧損，惟本集團已產生法定或推定責任或
          except to the extent that the Group has incurred legal              代表被投資公司作出付款則除外。就此而
          or constructive obligations or made payments on behalf              言，本集團的權益為根據權益法計量的投資
          of the investee. For this purpose, the Group’s interest is          的賬面值，連同實質上構成本集團於聯營公

      initially recognized at the present value of the lease             租賃付款現值確認，並按租賃中所隱含的利
      payments payable over the lease term, discounted                   率貼現，或倘該利率無法即時釐定，則以相
      using the interest rate implicit in the lease or, if that          關增量借款利率貼現。於初步確認後，租賃
      rate cannot be readily determined, using a relevant                負債按攤銷成本計量，而利息開支則採用實
      incremental borrowing rate. After initial recognition,             際利率法確認。租賃負債的計量並不包括不
      the lease liability is measured at amortized cost and              依賴指數或利率的可變租賃付款，因此可變
      interest expense is recognized using the effective                 租賃付款在產生時於損益扣除。
      interest method. Variable lease payments that do not
      depend on an index or rate are not included in the
      measurement of the lease liability and are charged to
      profit or loss as incurred.


      The right-of-use asset recognized when a lease                     於租賃資本化時確認的使用權資產初步按成
      is capitalized is initially measured at cost, which                本計量，包括就於開始日期或之前作出的任
      comprises the initial amount of the lease liability                何租賃付款調整的租賃負債的初始金額，加
      adjusted for any lease payments made at or before                  上所產生的任何初始直接成本及拆卸及移除
      the commencement date, plus any initial direct                     相關資產或恢復相關資產或其所在地原貌的
      costs incurred and an estimate of costs to dismantle               估計成本，減任何已收租賃優惠。使用權資
      and remove the underlying asset or to restore the                  產隨後按成本減去累計折舊及減值虧損列賬
      underlying asset or the site on which it is located,              （請參閱附註2(i)及2(l)(ii)）。
      less any lease incentives received. The right-of-use
      asset is subsequently stated at cost less accumulated
      depreciation and impairment losses (see Notes 2(i) and
      2(l)(ii)).

       losses. Generally, credit losses are measured as the                 損。信貸虧損通常按合約及預期金額之間的
       present value of all expected cash shortfalls between                所有預期現金差額的現值計量。
       the contractual and expected amounts.

               and


       –       variable-rate financial assets: current effective            －         浮動利率金融資產：當前的實際利
               interest rate.                                                         率。

      instrument (including a loan commitment) has                          自初始確認後是否顯著增加及計量預期信貸
      increased significantly since initial recognition and                 虧損時，本集團考慮無須付出不必要的額外

      and supportable information that is relevant and                      信息。這包括根據本集團的過往經驗及已知
      available without undue cost or effort. This includes                 信貸評估得出的定量及定性資料及分析，並
      both quantitative and qualitative information and                     包括前瞻性資料。
      analysis, based on the Group’s historical experience
      and informed credit assessment, that includes
      forward-looking information.


      The Group assumes that the credit risk on a financial                 本集團假設，倘金融資產逾期超過30天，其
      asset has increased significantly if it is more than 30               信貸風險會顯著增加。
      days past due.

      when:

      Credit-impaired financial assets                                    信貸減值金融資產


      At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether a                於各報告日期，本集團評估金融資產有否出
      financial asset is credit-impaired. A financial asset is            現信貸減值。當發生一項或多項對金融資產
      credit-impaired when one or more events that have a                 估計未來現金流量產生不利影響的事件時，
      detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows               金融資產即被視為出現信貸減值。
      of the financial asset have occurred.


      Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired                  金融資產出現信貸減值的跡象包括以下可觀
      includes the following observable events:                           察事件：


      –      significant financial difficulties of the debtor;            －         債務人面臨重大財務困難；


      –      a breach of contract, such as a default or                   －         違反合約，如利息或本金付款違約或
             delinquency in interest or principal payments;                         拖欠；


      –      the restructuring of a loan or advance by the                －         本集團根據其他情況下不會考慮的條
             Group on terms that the Group would not                                款重組貸款或墊款；
             consider otherwise;


      –      it is probable that the debtor will enter into               －         債務人有可能破產或需要進行其他財
             bankruptcy or other financial reorganization; or                       務重組；或


      –      the disappearance of an active market for a                  －         發行人陷入財務困難導致證券失去活
             security because of financial difficulties of the                      躍市場。
             issuer.


      Write-off policy                                                    撇銷政策


      The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is written           倘日後回收不可實現，金融資產的賬面總值
      off to the extent that there is no realistic prospect of            將撇銷。該情況通常出現在本集團以其他方
      recovery. This is generally the case when the Group                 式釐定債務人並無資產或收入來源可產生足
      otherwise determines that the debtor does not have                  夠現金流量以償還撇銷金額時。
      assets or sources of income that could generate
      sufficient cash flows to repay the amounts subject to
      the write-off.


      Subsequent recoveries of an asset that was previously               過往撇銷資產的後續回收於回收期間於損益
      written off are recognized as a reversal of impairment in           確認為減值撥回。
      profit or loss in the period in which the recovery occurs.

 (ii)     Impairment of other non-current assets                         (ii)   其他非流動資產減值

          At each reporting date, the Group reviews the                         於各報告日期，本集團審閱其非金融資產
          carrying amounts of its non-financial assets (other                   （存貨及遞延稅項資產除外）的賬面值，以釐
          than inventories and deferred tax assets) to determine                定是否有任何減值跡象。倘存在任何有關跡
          whether there is any indication of impairment. If any                 象，則會估計資產的可收回金額。每年對商
          such indication exists, then the asset’s recoverable                  譽進行減值測試。
          amount is estimated. Goodwill is tested annually for
          impairment.


          For impairment testing, assets are grouped together                   就減值測試而言，資產納入持續使用產生現
          into the smallest group of assets that generates cash                 金流入的最小組別，該組別基本獨立於其他
          inflows from continuing use that are largely independent              資產或現金產生單位（「現金產生單位」）的現
          of the cash inflows of other assets or cash-generating                金流入。業務合併產生的商譽分配至預期受
          units (“CGU”s). Goodwill arising from a business                      益於合併協同效應的現金產生單位或現金產
          combination is allocated to CGUs or groups of CGUs                    生單位組別。
          that are expected to benefit from the synergies of the
          combination.


          The recoverable amount of an asset or CGU is the                      資產或現金產生單位的可收回金額為其使用
          greater of its value in use and its fair value less costs of          價值與其公允價值減出售成本兩者中的較高
          disposal. Value in use is based on the estimated future               者。使用價值乃基於估計未來現金流量，使
          cash flows, discounted to their present value using                   用能反映現時市場對金錢的時間價值的評估
          a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market                  及該資產或現金產生單位的特有風險的除稅
          assessments of the time value of money and the risks                  前折現率折減至其現值。
          specific to the asset or CGU.


          An impairment loss is recognized if the carrying amount               倘資產或現金產生單位的賬面值超過其可收
          of an asset or CGU exceeds its recoverable amount.                    回金額，則確認減值虧損。


          Impairment losses are recognized in profit or loss. They              減值虧損於損益內確認。減值虧損首先用以
          are allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of any              調低分攤至現金產生單位的任何商譽的賬面
          goodwill allocated to the CGU, and then to reduce the                 值，然後按比例調低現金產生單位內其他資
          carrying amounts of the other assets in the CGU on a                  產的賬面值。
          pro rata basis.


          An impairment loss in respect of goodwill is not                      有關商譽之減值虧損將不予撥回。就其他資
          reversed. For other assets, an impairment loss is                     產而言，僅當所產生的賬面值不超過假設並
          reversed only to the extent that the resulting carrying               無確認減值虧損而應釐定的賬面值（已扣除
          amount does not exceed the carrying amount that                       折舊或攤銷）方可撥回減值虧損。
          would have been determined, net of depreciation
          or amortization, if no impairment loss had been
          recognized.

        normal operating capacity.

        the sale.

      extent that it is no longer probable that the related tax

      when the probability of future taxable profits improves.


      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset only if                   遞延稅項資產及負債僅於符合若干標準時方
      certain criteria are met.                                                可抵銷。

 (v)      Provisions and contingent liabilities                         (v)   撥備及或有負債

          Generally provisions are determined by discounting the              撥備通常通過按反映當時市場對貨幣時間價
          expected future cash flows at a pre-tax rate that reflects          值及負債特定風險的評估的除稅前利率貼現
          current market assessment of the time value of money                預期未來現金流量而釐定。
          and the risks specific to the liability.


          A provision for warranties is recognized when the                   保修撥備乃於相關產品或服務售出時根據過
          underlying products or services are sold, based on                  往保修數據及權衡保修可能出現的結果及有
          historical warranty data and a weighting of possible                關的可能性確認。
          outcomes against their associated probabilities.


          A provision for onerous contracts is measured at the                多項虧損性合約的撥備乃按終止合約預期成
          present value of the lower of the expected cost of                  本及繼續合約預期成本淨額（以較低者為準）
          terminating the contract and the expected net cost of               的現值計量，而繼續合約預期成本淨額乃根
          continuing with the contract, which is determined based             據履行該合約項下責任的增量成本及與履行
          on the incremental costs of fulfilling the obligation under         該合約直接相關的其他成本的分配釐定。於
          that contract and an allocation of other costs directly             計提撥備前，本集團就該合約相關資產確認
          related to fulfilling that contract. Before a provision is          任何減值虧損（請參閱附註2(l)(ii)）。
          established, the Group recognizes any impairment loss
          on the assets associated with that contract (see Note 2
          (l)(ii)).


          A restructuring provision is recognised only when the               重組撥備於符合撥備的一般確認標準時方獲
          general recognition criteria for provision are met. The             確認。重組責任往往為推定責任。推定重組
          obligation for a restructuring is often constructive. A             責任於出現下列情況時方會產生：詳盡的正
          constructive restructuring obligation arises only when              式計劃識別出主要重組特性；及對該等受影
          there is a detailed formal plan identifying the main                響者合理預期實體將開始執行計劃或向該等
          features of the restructuring; and a valid expectation              受影響者公佈主要特性以進行重組。
          in those affected that the entity will carry out the
          restructuring by starting to implement the plan or
          announcing its main features to those affected.


          Restructuring provision includes only the direct                    重組撥備僅包括源自重組的直接開支，該項
          expenditures arising from the restructuring, which are              開支屬重組的必要開支，而非有關實體持續
          necessarily entailed by the restructuring and not those             活動的開支。於計量重組撥備時概無計及任
          associated with the entity’s ongoing activities. Any                何資產銷售的預期收益。
          expected gains on the sale of assets are not considered
          in measuring a restructuring provision.

          (b)       Interest income                                               (b)   利息收益

                    Interest income is recognized using the effective                    利息收益按實際利率法確認。「實際
                    interest method. The ‘effective interest rate’ is                    利率」指將金融資產於預計年期內的
                    the rate that exactly discounts estimated future                     估計未來現金收入準確貼現至金融
                    cash receipts through the expected life of the                       資產總賬面值的利率。計算利息收益
                    financial asset to the gross carrying amount of                      時，實際利率應用於資產的總賬面值
                    the financial asset. In calculating interest income,                （ 倘 資 產 並 無 信 貸 減 值 ）。 然 而 ， 對
                    the effective interest rate is applied to the gross                  於初始確認後出現信貸減值的金融資
                    carrying amount of the asset (when the asset is                      產，利息收益通過對金融資產的攤銷
                    not credit-impaired). However, for financial assets                  成本應用實際利率計算。倘資產不再
                    that have become credit-impaired subsequent to                       出現信貸減值，則利息收益的計算將
                    initial recognition, interest income is calculated                   恢復至總額基準。
                    by applying the effective interest rate to the
                    amortized cost of the financial asset. If the asset
                    is no longer credit-impaired, then the calculation
                    of interest income reverts to the gross basis.

                    incurred are recognized as income in profit or                      產生的相同期間有系統地於損益確認
                    loss on a systematic basis in the same periods                      為收益。

(z)    Borrowing costs                                                (z)     借貸成本

       Borrowing costs that directly attributable to the                      資產的收購、建設或生產（須經過頗長時間
       acquisition, construction or production of an asset                    籌備以作擬定用途或出售）直接應佔借貸成
       which necessarily takes a substantial period of time to                本撥充為該資產的部分成本。其他借貸成本
       get ready for its intended use or sale are capitalized as              於產生期間支銷。
       part of the cost of that asset. Other borrowing costs are
       expensed in the period in which they are incurred.


(aa)   Asset acquisition                                              (aa)    資產收購

       Groups of assets acquired and liabilities assumed are                  對所收購的資產組別及所承擔的負債進行評
       assessed to determine if they are business or asset                    估，以釐定其為業務或資產收購。按逐項收
       acquisitions. On an acquisition-by-acquisition basis,                  購基準，當所收購的資產總值的大部分公允
       the Group chooses to apply a simplified assessment                     價值都集中於單一的可辨認資產或類似可辨
       of whether an acquired set of activities and assets                    認資產組別中時，本集團選擇採用簡化的評
       is an asset rather than business acquisition, when                     估方法以釐定所收購的一組業務及資產是否
       substantially all of the fair value of the gross assets                為資產而非業務收購。
       acquired is concentrated in a single identifiable asset
       or group of similar identifiable assets.


       When a group of assets acquired and liabilities                        當所收購的一組資產及所承擔的負債不構成
       assumed do not constitute a business, the overall                      業務時，按其於收購日期的相對公允價值，
       acquisition cost is allocated to the individual identifiable           將整體收購成本分攤至單項可識別資產及負
       assets and liabilities based on their relative fair values             債。可識別資產及負債的個別公允價值之和
       at the date of acquisition. An exception is when the sum               不同於整體收購成本時除外。於該等情況
       of the individual fair values of the identifiable assets               下，任何根據本集團的政策按成本以外的金
       and liabilities differs from the overall acquisition cost.             額進行初始計量的可識別資產及負債均應進
       In such case, any identifiable assets and liabilities that             行相應計量，剩餘收購成本根據其於收購日
       are initially measured at an amount other than cost in                 期的相對公允價值分配至剩餘可識別資產及
       accordance with the Group’s policies are measured                      負債。
       accordingly, and the residual acquisition cost is
       allocated to the remaining identifiable assets and
       liabilities based on their relative fair values at the date
       of acquisition.

     the increased risk of investing in equities generally

     Accordingly, no impairment loss for goodwill was                 的減值虧損。此外，基於上述敏感度分析，
     recognised in the consolidated statements of profit or           本集團確定，於 2025 年及 2024 年 12 月 31
     loss. Also, based on the sensitivity analysis above, the         日，關鍵參數的合理可能變動不會導致現金
     Group concluded that a reasonably possible change in             產生單位的賬面值超過其可收回金額。
     key parameters would not cause the carrying amount
     of the CGU to exceed its recoverable amount as at
     December 31, 2025 and 2024.

      to receive the full face amount from the banks at                  承兌票據，該等票據通常自發行日期起為期
      maturity, which generally ranges from 3 to 12 months               3 至12 個月。於過往，本集團未遇到應收票
      from the date of issuance. Historically, the Group had             據出現信貸虧損的情況。
      experienced no credit losses on bills receivable.

          then settles with the banks between 90 – 120 days,               有關安排產生的利息由供應商承擔。
          which are the original due dates with the suppliers,
          without interest. The interest arising from such
          arrangements are born by the suppliers.


          In the consolidated statement of financial position, the         於綜合財務狀況表內，鑒於有關負債的性質
          Group has presented the payables to the banks under              及功能與本集團應付供應商的貿易應付款項
          these arrangements as “trade and bills payables”, in             類似及該等負債為本集團於正常運營週期所
          view of the nature and function of such liabilities are          用的營運資金的一部分，本集團已將根據該
          similar to the Group’s trade payables to suppliers, and          等安排應付銀行的款項呈列為「貿易應付款
          these liabilities are part of the working capital used in        項及應付票據」。於 2025 年 12 月 31 日，該
          the Group’s normal operating cycle. As at December               等安排項下的金融負債賬面值計入「貿易應

(e)   Capital management                                          (e)    資本管理

      The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital               本集團管理資本的首要目標乃保障本集團能
      are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a              夠持續經營，以便透過對與風險水平相應的
      going concern, so that it can continue to provide returns          產品及服務定價以及獲得合理成本的融資，
      for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders,              繼續為股東創造回報及為其他持份者帶來利
      by pricing products and services commensurately with               益。
      the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a
      reasonable cost.


      The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages               本集團定期積極對資本架構進行檢討及管
      its capital structure to maintaining a balance between             理，以在較高股東回報情況下可能伴隨的較
      the higher shareholders returns that might be possible             高借貸水平與良好資本狀況帶來的好處及保
      with higher levels of borrowings and the advantages                障之間取得平衡，並根據經濟狀況的變化對
      and security afforded by a sound capital position, and             資本結構作出調整。
      makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
      changes in economic conditions.


      The Group monitors its capital structure on the basis of           本集團利用淨資本負債比率監察其資本結
      a net gearing ratio. For this purpose, the Group defines           構。就此而言，本集團將淨負債界定為流動
      net debt as total current and non-current bank loans               及非流動銀行貸款及租賃負債總額減現金及
      and lease liabilities less cash and cash equivalents               現金等價物及已抵押存款。本集團將資本界
      and pledged deposits. The Group defines capital as                 定為包括所有權益組成部分。
      including all components of equity.

33    FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT AND FAIR                            33     財務風險管理及金融工具的公允價值
      VALUES OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS
      Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency            本集團業務面臨正常業務過程中產生的信
      risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s                     貸、流動性、利率及貨幣風險。
      business.


      The Group’s exposure to these risks and financial risk               本集團面臨的該等風險及本集團為管理該等
      management policies and practices used by the Group                  風險而採用的財務風險管理政策及慣例描述
      to manage these risks are described below:                           如下：


(a)   Credit risk                                                   (a)    信貸風險

      Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will              信貸風險指對手方將違反合約義務而導致本
      default on its contractual obligations resulting in a                集團產生財務虧損的風險。本集團信貸風險
      financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is              主要歸因於貿易及其他應收款項。本集團面
      primarily attributable to trade and other receivables.               對來自現金及現金等價物、已抵押存款及應
      The Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash                收票據的信貸風險有限，原因是對手方為信
      and cash equivalents, pledged deposits and bills                     譽狀況良好的知名金融機構，本集團認為其
      receivable is limited because the counterparties are                 信貸風險較低。
      reputable financial institutions with high credit standing,
      for which the Group considers to have low credit risk.


      Except for the financial guarantees given by the Group               除附註35所載由本集團提供的財務擔保外，
      as set out in Note 35 the Group does not provide any                 本集團並無任何其他將令本集團承受信貸風
      other guarantees which would expose the Group to                     險的擔保。於報告期末有關該等財務擔保的
      credit risk. The maximum exposure to credit risk in                  最高信貸風險敞口乃披露於附註35。
      respect of these financial guarantees at the end of the
      reporting period is disclosed in Note 35.


      Trade receivables                                                    貿易應收款項

      The Group has established a credit risk management                   本集團已制定信貸風險管理政策，據此對所
      policy under which individual credit evaluations are                 有需要超過一定金額的信貸的客戶均進行個
      performed on all customers requiring credit over                     別信貸評估。該等評估集中於客戶過往到期
      a certain amount. These evaluations focus on the                     作出付款的記錄及目前的支付能力，並計及
      customer’s past history of making payments when                      客戶的特定資料以及有關客戶經營所在經濟
      due and current ability to pay and take into account                 環境的資料。貿易應收款項自賬單日期起計
      information specific to the customer as well as                      30 至180 日內到期。正常情況下，本集團不
      pertaining to the economic environment in which the                  會收取客戶的抵押品。
      customer operates. Trade receivables are due within
      30 to 180 days from the date of billing. Normally, the
      Group does not obtain collateral from customers.

 (b)      Liquidity risk                                                (b)     流動性風險

          Individual operating entities within the Group are                    本集團旗下個別經營實體負責各自現金管
          responsible for their own cash management, including                  理，包括現金盈餘短期投資及為滿足預期現
          the short-term investment of cash surpluses and the                   金要求而籌集貸款，惟借款超出一定授權預
          raising of loans to cover expected cash demands,                      定水平後須取得母公司董事會批准。本集團
          subject to approval by the parent company’s board                     的政策為定期監察其流動資金需求及其遵守
          when the borrowings exceed certain predetermined                      借貸契諾的情況，以確保維持充足的現金儲
          levels of authority. The Group’s policy is to regularly               備及來自主要金融機構的足夠承諾額度以滿
          monitor its liquidity requirements and its compliance                 足其短期及長期的流動資金需求。
          with lending covenants to ensure that it maintains
          sufficient reserves of cash and adequate committed
          lines of funding from major financial institutions to meet
          its liquidity requirements in the short and longer term.

          loans that are denominated in a currency other than                 項、應付款項、現金結餘及銀行貸款。引起
          the functional currency of the operations to which the              該風險的貨幣主要為美元及歐元。
          transactions relate. The currencies giving rise to this
          risk are primarily USD and EUR.

      The following table presents the fair value of the                    下表列示於各報告期末按經常基準計量本集
      Group’s financial instruments measured at the                         團金融工具的公允價值，並歸入香港財務報
      end of each reporting period on a recurring basis,                    告準則第13號公允價值計量 界定的三個公允
      categorized into the three-level fair value hierarchy as              價值層級。公允價值計量分類層級乃參考估
      defined in HKFRS 13, Fair value measurement . The                     值方法中所使用輸入數據的可觀察及重要性
      level into which a fair value measurement is classified               釐定如下：
      is determined with reference to the observability
      and significance of the inputs used in the valuation
      technique as follows:

      –      Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using                  －       第一級估值：僅採用第一級輸入數據
             only Level 1 inputs i.e. unadjusted quoted prices                      （即相同資產或負債於計量日期在活
             in active markets for identical assets or liabilities                  躍市場上未經調整的報價）計量的公
             at the measurement date;                                               允價值；

      –      Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using                  －       第二級估值：採用第二級輸入數據
             Level 2 inputs i.e. observable inputs which                            （即不滿足第一級要求的可觀察輸入
             fail to meet Level 1, and not using significant                        數據）及並無採用重大不可觀察輸入
             unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs are                           數據計量的公允價值。不可觀察輸入
             inputs for which market data are not available;                        數據為無法獲得市場數據的輸入數
                                                                                    據；

      –      Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using                  －         第三級估值：採用重大不可觀察輸入
             significant unobservable inputs.                                         數據計量的公允價值。

          statements and aims to improve the transparency and                        則第 1 號財務報表的呈列，並旨在提高實體

          Among other changes, under HKFRS 18, entities are                          除其他變動外，根據香港財務報告準則第18

          operations and income tax categories. Entities are                         單獨附註中具體披露有關管理層定義的績效
          also required to provide specific disclosures about                        指標。
